Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The algebra variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 491 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 886 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the algebra space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 491 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 886. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The algebra operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 461. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 12 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 461 + O(1/n^12). Summing over all modes and applying the algebra structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
